    \section{Orbitals And Their Symmetry}%Orbitale und deren Symmetrie 

%\includegraphics[scale=0.125]{img/DimerZOrbitalordnung-gesamt-MO-C6H6-beiWW.pdf}
\vspace{2cm}
We use the following ordering of orbitals here (assuming degeneracy of LUMOs and HOMOs): 

$$\begin{tikzpicture}[baseline={(current bounding box.center)}]
% lower MOs:
\draw[thick] (0,-0.6) -- (0.5,-0.6)node[pos=0, left] {$b_{2g}$}
;;\draw[thick] (1,-0.6) -- (1.5,-0.6)node[pos=1, right] {$b_{3g}$}
;;
% upper MOs:
\draw[thick] (0,0) -- (0.5,0)node[pos=0, left] {$b_{1u}$}
;;\draw[thick] (1,0) -- (1.5,0)node[pos=1, right] {$a_{u}$}
;;
\end{tikzpicture}$$
In the following, we will leave out the explicit labeling by orbital symmetry. Each monomer will simply be written as:

$$\begin{tikzpicture}[baseline={(current bounding box.center)}]
% lower MOs:
\draw[thick] (0,-0.6) -- (0.5,-0.6)%node[pos=0, left] {$b_{2g}$}
;;\draw[thick] (1,-0.6) -- (1.5,-0.6)%node[pos=1, right] {$b_{3g}$}
;;
% upper MOs:
\draw[thick] (0,0) -- (0.5,0)%node[pos=0, left] {$b_{1u}$}
;;\draw[thick] (1,0) -- (1.5,0)%node[pos=1, right] {$a_{u}$}
;;
\end{tikzpicture}$$
where the character of the orbital is defined by its position. \\ \\ 


Dimer configurations will be written as their monomer occupations, by writing one monomer to the left and one to the right. The orbital ordering within the group of monomer orbitals follows the above mentioned definition. \\ \\ The transformation of monomer orbitals into dimer orbitals is given by knowing the negative and positive linear combinations of monomer orbitals into dimer orbitals. Transforming this known equation system leads to: 
\begin{subequations}\begin{gather*}
a_{u}^{\mathsf{l}} = +b_{1g}+a_{u}  \\
b_{1u}^{\mathsf{l}} = +a_{g}+b_{1u}  \\
b_{2g}^{\mathsf{l}} = +b_{3u}+b_{2g}  \\
b_{3g}^{\mathsf{l}} = +b_{2u}+b_{3g}  \\
a_{u}^{\mathsf{r}} = -b_{1g}+a_{u}  \\
b_{1u}^{\mathsf{r}} = -a_{g}+b_{1u}  \\
b_{2g}^{\mathsf{r}} = -b_{3u}+b_{2g}  \\
b_{3g}^{\mathsf{r}} = -b_{2u}+b_{3g}
\end{gather*}\end{subequations}

monomer ci vectors:
$$\begin{array}{c}
+\left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{2}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{2}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{0}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{0}}_{a_{g}^{\mathsf{l}}}\right| \bullet{} \left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}}_{a_{g}^{\mathsf{r}}}\right| +\left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}}_{a_{g}^{\mathsf{l}}}\right| \bullet{} \left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{2}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{2}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{0}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{0}}_{a_{g}^{\mathsf{r}}}\right|
\end{array}$$substitution by dimer orbitals:
$$+\left|\underbrace{(+b_{2u}+b_{3g})^{2}(+b_{3u}+b_{2g})^{2}(+a_{g}+b_{1u})^{0}(+b_{1g}+a_{u})^{0}}_{a_{g}^{\mathsf{l}}}\right| \bullet{} \left|\underbrace{(-b_{2u}+b_{3g})^{1}(-b_{3u}+b_{2g})^{1}(-a_{g}+b_{1u})^{1}(-b_{1g}+a_{u})^{1}}_{a_{g}^{\mathsf{r}}}\right| + \hdots $$(multiplied out and summed up) dimer ci vectors:

\begin{multline*}\begin{array}{c}
+2 \cdot \left|\underbrace{(b_{2u})^{2}(b_{3g})^{1}(b_{3u})^{2}(b_{2g})^{1}(a_{g})^{0}(b_{1u})^{1}(b_{1g})^{0}(a_{u})^{1}}_{a_{g}}\right| +2 \cdot \left|\underbrace{(b_{2u})^{2}(b_{3g})^{1}(b_{3u})^{2}(b_{2g})^{1}(a_{g})^{1}(b_{1u})^{0}(b_{1g})^{1}(a_{u})^{0}}_{a_{g}}\right| \\[0.5cm] +2 \cdot \left|\underbrace{(b_{2u})^{2}(b_{3g})^{1}(b_{3u})^{1}(b_{2g})^{2}(a_{g})^{0}(b_{1u})^{1}(b_{1g})^{1}(a_{u})^{0}}_{a_{g}}\right| +2 \cdot \left|\underbrace{(b_{2u})^{2}(b_{3g})^{1}(b_{3u})^{1}(b_{2g})^{2}(a_{g})^{1}(b_{1u})^{0}(b_{1g})^{0}(a_{u})^{1}}_{a_{g}}\right| \\[0.5cm] +2 \cdot \left|\underbrace{(b_{2u})^{1}(b_{3g})^{2}(b_{3u})^{2}(b_{2g})^{1}(a_{g})^{0}(b_{1u})^{1}(b_{1g})^{1}(a_{u})^{0}}_{a_{g}}\right| +2 \cdot \left|\underbrace{(b_{2u})^{1}(b_{3g})^{2}(b_{3u})^{2}(b_{2g})^{1}(a_{g})^{1}(b_{1u})^{0}(b_{1g})^{0}(a_{u})^{1}}_{a_{g}}\right| \\[0.5cm] +2 \cdot \left|\underbrace{(b_{2u})^{1}(b_{3g})^{2}(b_{3u})^{1}(b_{2g})^{2}(a_{g})^{0}(b_{1u})^{1}(b_{1g})^{0}(a_{u})^{1}}_{a_{g}}\right| +2 \cdot \left|\underbrace{(b_{2u})^{1}(b_{3g})^{2}(b_{3u})^{1}(b_{2g})^{2}(a_{g})^{1}(b_{1u})^{0}(b_{1g})^{1}(a_{u})^{0}}_{a_{g}}\right|
\end{array}\end{multline*}

+\left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{2}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{0}}_{b_{2u}^{\mathsf{l}}}\right| \bullet{} +\left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{2}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{0}}_{b_{2u}^{\mathsf{l}}}\right| \bullet{} \left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{2}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{0}}_{b_{2u}^{\mathsf{r}}}\right| \\[0.5cm] \left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{2}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{0}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}}_{b_{2u}^{\mathsf{r}}}\right| +\left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{2}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{0}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}}_{b_{2u}^{\mathsf{l}}}\right| \bullet{} \left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{2}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{0}}_{b_{2u}^{\mathsf{r}}}\right| \\[0.5cm] -\left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{2}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{0}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}}_{b_{2u}^{\mathsf{l}}}\right| \bullet{} \left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{2}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{0}}_{b_{2u}^{\mathsf{r}}}\right| -\left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{2}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{0}}_{b_{2u}^{\mathsf{l}}}\right| \bullet{} \left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{2}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{0}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}}_{b_{2u}^{\mathsf{r}}}\right| \\[0.5cm] -\left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{2}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{0}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}}_{b_{2u}^{\mathsf{l}}}\right| \bullet{} \left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{2}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{0}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}}_{b_{2u}^{\mathsf{r}}}\right| -\left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{2}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{0}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}}_{b_{2u}^{\mathsf{l}}}\right| \bullet{} \left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{2}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{0}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}}_{b_{2u}^{\mathsf{r}}}\right|

$$+\left|\underbrace{(+b_{2u}+b_{3g})^{1}(+b_{3u}+b_{2g})^{2}(+a_{g}+b_{1u})^{1}(+b_{1g}+a_{u})^{0}}_{b_{2u}^{\mathsf{l}}}\right| \bullet{} \left|\underbrace{(-b_{2u}+b_{3g})^{1}(-b_{3u}+b_{2g})^{2}(-a_{g}+b_{1u})^{1}(-b_{1g}+a_{u})^{0}}_{b_{2u}^{\mathsf{r}}}\right| + \hdots $$(multiplied out and summed up) dimer ci vectors:

\begin{multline*}\begin{array}{c}
+2 \cdot \left|\underbrace{(b_{2u})^{1}(b_{3g})^{1}(b_{3u})^{2}(b_{2g})^{2}(a_{g})^{1}(b_{1u})^{1}(b_{1g})^{0}(a_{u})^{0}}_{a_{g}}\right| -2 \cdot \left|\underbrace{(b_{2u})^{2}(b_{3g})^{2}(b_{3u})^{1}(b_{2g})^{1}(a_{g})^{0}(b_{1u})^{0}(b_{1g})^{1}(a_{u})^{1}}_{a_{g}}\right|
\end{array}\end{multline*}

$$\begin{array}{c}
+\left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{2}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{0}}_{b_{2u}^{\mathsf{l}}}\right| \bullet{} \left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{2}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{0}}_{b_{2u}^{\mathsf{r}}}\right| +\left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{2}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{0}}_{b_{2u}^{\mathsf{l}}}\right| \bullet{} \left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{2}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{0}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}}_{b_{2u}^{\mathsf{r}}}\right| \\[0.5cm] +\left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{2}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{0}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}}_{b_{2u}^{\mathsf{l}}}\right| \bullet{} \left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{2}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{0}}_{b_{2u}^{\mathsf{r}}}\right| +\left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{2}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{0}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}}_{b_{2u}^{\mathsf{l}}}\right| \bullet{} \left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{2}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{0}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}}_{b_{2u}^{\mathsf{r}}}\right|
\end{array}$$substitution by dimer orbitals:
$$+\left|\underbrace{(+b_{2u}+b_{3g})^{1}(+b_{3u}+b_{2g})^{2}(+a_{g}+b_{1u})^{1}(+b_{1g}+a_{u})^{0}}_{b_{2u}^{\mathsf{l}}}\right| \bullet{} \left|\underbrace{(-b_{2u}+b_{3g})^{1}(-b_{3u}+b_{2g})^{2}(-a_{g}+b_{1u})^{1}(-b_{1g}+a_{u})^{0}}_{b_{2u}^{\mathsf{r}}}\right| + \hdots $$(multiplied out and summed up) dimer ci vectors:

\begin{multline*}\begin{array}{c}
+\left|\underbrace{(b_{2u})^{1}(b_{3g})^{1}(b_{3u})^{2}(b_{2g})^{2}(a_{g})^{1}(b_{1u})^{1}(b_{1g})^{0}(a_{u})^{0}}_{a_{g}}\right| -2 \cdot \left|\underbrace{(b_{2u})^{1}(b_{3g})^{2}(b_{3u})^{2}(b_{2g})^{1}(a_{g})^{1}(b_{1u})^{0}(b_{1g})^{0}(a_{u})^{1}}_{a_{g}}\right| \\[0.5cm] +2 \cdot \left|\underbrace{(b_{2u})^{1}(b_{3g})^{2}(b_{3u})^{2}(b_{2g})^{1}(a_{g})^{0}(b_{1u})^{1}(b_{1g})^{1}(a_{u})^{0}}_{a_{g}}\right| -2 \cdot \left|\underbrace{(b_{2u})^{1}(b_{3g})^{2}(b_{3u})^{1}(b_{2g})^{2}(a_{g})^{1}(b_{1u})^{0}(b_{1g})^{1}(a_{u})^{0}}_{a_{g}}\right| \\[0.5cm] +2 \cdot \left|\underbrace{(b_{2u})^{1}(b_{3g})^{2}(b_{3u})^{1}(b_{2g})^{2}(a_{g})^{0}(b_{1u})^{1}(b_{1g})^{0}(a_{u})^{1}}_{a_{g}}\right| +2 \cdot \left|\underbrace{(b_{2u})^{2}(b_{3g})^{1}(b_{3u})^{2}(b_{2g})^{1}(a_{g})^{1}(b_{1u})^{0}(b_{1g})^{1}(a_{u})^{0}}_{a_{g}}\right| \\[0.5cm] -2 \cdot \left|\underbrace{(b_{2u})^{2}(b_{3g})^{1}(b_{3u})^{2}(b_{2g})^{1}(a_{g})^{0}(b_{1u})^{1}(b_{1g})^{0}(a_{u})^{1}}_{a_{g}}\right| +2 \cdot \left|\underbrace{(b_{2u})^{2}(b_{3g})^{1}(b_{3u})^{1}(b_{2g})^{2}(a_{g})^{1}(b_{1u})^{0}(b_{1g})^{0}(a_{u})^{1}}_{a_{g}}\right| \\[0.5cm] -2 \cdot \left|\underbrace{(b_{2u})^{2}(b_{3g})^{1}(b_{3u})^{1}(b_{2g})^{2}(a_{g})^{0}(b_{1u})^{1}(b_{1g})^{1}(a_{u})^{0}}_{a_{g}}\right| +\left|\underbrace{(b_{2u})^{2}(b_{3g})^{2}(b_{3u})^{1}(b_{2g})^{1}(a_{g})^{0}(b_{1u})^{0}(b_{1g})^{1}(a_{u})^{1}}_{a_{g}}\right|
\end{array}\end{multline*}

$$\begin{array}{c}
+\left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{2}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{0}}_{b_{3u}^{\mathsf{l}}}\right| \bullet{} \left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{2}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{0}}_{b_{3u}^{\mathsf{r}}}\right| -\left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{2}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{0}}_{b_{3u}^{\mathsf{l}}}\right| \bullet{} \left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{2}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{0}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}}_{b_{3u}^{\mathsf{r}}}\right| \\[0.5cm] -\left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{2}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{0}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}}_{b_{3u}^{\mathsf{l}}}\right| \bullet{} \left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{2}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{0}}_{b_{3u}^{\mathsf{r}}}\right| +\left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{l}})^{2}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{0}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{l}})^{1}}_{b_{3u}^{\mathsf{l}}}\right| \bullet{} \left|\underbrace{(b_{3g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}(b_{2g}^{\mathsf{r}})^{2}(b_{1u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{0}(a_{u}^{\mathsf{r}})^{1}}_{b_{3u}^{\mathsf{r}}}\right|
\end{array}$$substitution by dimer orbitals:
$$+\left|\underbrace{(+b_{2u}+b_{3g})^{2}(+b_{3u}+b_{2g})^{1}(+a_{g}+b_{1u})^{1}(+b_{1g}+a_{u})^{0}}_{b_{3u}^{\mathsf{l}}}\right| \bullet{} \left|\underbrace{(-b_{2u}+b_{3g})^{2}(-b_{3u}+b_{2g})^{1}(-a_{g}+b_{1u})^{1}(-b_{1g}+a_{u})^{0}}_{b_{3u}^{\mathsf{r}}}\right| + \hdots $$(multiplied out and summed up) dimer ci vectors:

\begin{multline*}\begin{array}{c}
+\left|\underbrace{(b_{2u})^{2}(b_{3g})^{2}(b_{3u})^{1}(b_{2g})^{1}(a_{g})^{1}(b_{1u})^{1}(b_{1g})^{0}(a_{u})^{0}}_{a_{g}}\right| -2 \cdot \left|\underbrace{(b_{2u})^{2}(b_{3g})^{1}(b_{3u})^{1}(b_{2g})^{2}(a_{g})^{1}(b_{1u})^{0}(b_{1g})^{0}(a_{u})^{1}}_{a_{g}}\right| \\[0.5cm] +2 \cdot \left|\underbrace{(b_{2u})^{2}(b_{3g})^{1}(b_{3u})^{1}(b_{2g})^{2}(a_{g})^{0}(b_{1u})^{1}(b_{1g})^{1}(a_{u})^{0}}_{a_{g}}\right| +2 \cdot \left|\underbrace{(b_{2u})^{2}(b_{3g})^{1}(b_{3u})^{2}(b_{2g})^{1}(a_{g})^{1}(b_{1u})^{0}(b_{1g})^{1}(a_{u})^{0}}_{a_{g}}\right| \\[0.5cm] -2 \cdot \left|\underbrace{(b_{2u})^{2}(b_{3g})^{1}(b_{3u})^{2}(b_{2g})^{1}(a_{g})^{0}(b_{1u})^{1}(b_{1g})^{0}(a_{u})^{1}}_{a_{g}}\right| -2 \cdot \left|\underbrace{(b_{2u})^{1}(b_{3g})^{2}(b_{3u})^{1}(b_{2g})^{2}(a_{g})^{1}(b_{1u})^{0}(b_{1g})^{1}(a_{u})^{0}}_{a_{g}}\right| \\[0.5cm] +2 \cdot \left|\underbrace{(b_{2u})^{1}(b_{3g})^{2}(b_{3u})^{1}(b_{2g})^{2}(a_{g})^{0}(b_{1u})^{1}(b_{1g})^{0}(a_{u})^{1}}_{a_{g}}\right| +2 \cdot \left|\underbrace{(b_{2u})^{1}(b_{3g})^{2}(b_{3u})^{2}(b_{2g})^{1}(a_{g})^{1}(b_{1u})^{0}(b_{1g})^{0}(a_{u})^{1}}_{a_{g}}\right| \\[0.5cm] -2 \cdot \left|\underbrace{(b_{2u})^{1}(b_{3g})^{2}(b_{3u})^{2}(b_{2g})^{1}(a_{g})^{0}(b_{1u})^{1}(b_{1g})^{1}(a_{u})^{0}}_{a_{g}}\right| +\left|\underbrace{(b_{2u})^{1}(b_{3g})^{1}(b_{3u})^{2}(b_{2g})^{2}(a_{g})^{0}(b_{1u})^{0}(b_{1g})^{1}(a_{u})^{1}}_{a_{g}}\right|
\end{array}\end{multline*}
